1. Instruction - Carol gives specific instructions regarding the timing of using "Funny Clown".
2. Disagreement - Ross disagrees with Carol about what they've previously discussed.
3. Statement Non-Opinion - Ross makes a factual statement about their enjoyable time together.
4. Statement Non-Opinion - Ross describes what they do together, no personal opinion is stated.
5. Statement Non-Opinion - Ross describes their relationship in a factual statement.
6. Opinion - Susan expresses her opinion that Ross is good with the child. She also states her opinion about herself.
7. Opinion - Rachel expresses a positive opinion about something being cute.
8. Statement Non-Opinion - Susan states that she bought something for the child.
9. Statement Non-Opinion - Ross comments on a clever statement.
10. Greeting - Monica greets someone over the phone.
11. Greeting - Monica greets the person she was calling with a more specific greeting.
12. Backchannel - Monica lists to the person on the phone.
13. Backchannel - Monica continues to listen to the person on the other end.
14. Agreement - Monica agrees to something suggested by the person she was calling.
15. Closing - Monica sets a future meeting time.
16. Greeting - Monica says goodbye, ending the phone call.
17. Question - Rachel asks Monica about something she heard her say in her greeting.
18. Answer - Monica responds to Rachel's question, giving clear context about her statement.
